# Worldwide sight reassessment — individual decision register

**Audit date: 22 November 2026.** This is a follow-on quality-control pass to the historic 1,733-sight migration. The earlier frozen benchmark profiles remain historical records, not current instructions to overwrite the newly revised YAML.

**1913 records individually enumerated and diagnostically screened**, including **980 sight-specific desk-based editorial decisions** (including **490 newly evidence-referenced reviews**), **935 numeric score changes**, and **933 automatically screened, unchanged records**.

### Important limitation

**Automated screening is not an independently researched individual assessment.** The user requested an item-by-item worldwide re-review; this audit records every item and applies all documented sight-specific judgments that could be completed. Sites marked `Automated screening only` retain provisional pre-existing scores and **still require individually researched verification**. No claim of 1,913 fresh independently researched destination reviews is made.

All core scores use the existing worldwide v2 five-dimension weights. No national score quotas, country-specific multipliers, 2026 migration curves, or newly approved 10/10 fields were used. Long-term significance does not imply admission or safety today.

## Review decisions and safeguards

A decision is entered only for a named attraction with five explicit updated core dimensions and a site-specific reason. These revised values are **subjective desk-based editorial judgments**, not proven facts or a replacement for future individual research.

Comparators are four published global ladder **starting points per sight type**, not proof that each individual destination has been independently investigated. The detailed site-specific rationale for targeted entries is shown for transparency.

**Open verification backlog:** 933 automatically screened sights, including 0 still scoring 90+.
